```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/portfolio/exhaustive.py
"""
Aggregated canonical portfolio ingest fixture.

Concatenates all per-instrument fixtures into one DataFrame for full
end-to-end ingest-pipeline coverage:

  - SPOT                           (uid 1..11)    -> spot.py
  - VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE           (uid 100..105) -> vanilla_forward_node.py
  - VANILLA_FORWARD_CURVE          (uid 200..205) -> vanilla_forward_curve.py
  - VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE   (uid 300..305) -> vanilla_european_option_node.py
  - VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE  (uid 400..404) -> vanilla_european_option_curve.py

uid partition is contiguous-per-instrument so a downstream test can
filter by instrument without re-reading the source files. Total rows:
11 + 6 + 6 + 6 + 5 = 34.

Coverage of resolution paths (against the canonical static + proxy
config fixtures):

  - DIRECT lookup (node + curve point underlyings)
  - RF-LEVEL PROXY      (USDGBP -> USDEUR, EURCHF -> USDEUR,
                          USD_LIBOR_3M[10Y] -> USD_SOFR_OIS[10Y])
  - CONTAINER PROXY     (WTI_CRUDE_FWD -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD,
                          HENRY_HUB_GAS_FWD -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD)
  - FX paths            (direct USDEUR; inverse settle currency;
                          RF-proxied USDGBP/EURCHF underlyings)
  - Discount basis      (OIS default vs explicit IBOR override)
  - Cross-curve discount (USD_SOFR_OIS / EUR_ESTR_OIS; USD_SOFR_OIS /
                          GBP_SONIA_OIS)
  - Netting linkage     (spot fixture; sets 101, 102, 103)
  - Option payoff sign  (calls and puts both present)
  - Date semantics      (expiry + delivery + settlement combinations)

Registered-but-not-exercised proxy (documented gap):
  POWER_CH_BASELOAD_HY -> CO2_EUA_ANNUAL_FWD
    Container proxy across mismatched x_axis_kinds (OFFSET_HY -> OFFSET_Y).
    The current resolver requires same-kind axes; resolving across kinds
    is a separate work item. The proxy is registered in the canonical
    proxy_config fixture as a *catalogue entry*, but no ingest row here
    consumes it — doing so would raise PrettyKeyError at resolution time.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import cast

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.portfolio.spot import (
    CANONICAL_SPOT_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA,
)
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.portfolio.vanilla_european_option_curve import (
    CANONICAL_VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA,
)
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.portfolio.vanilla_european_option_node import (
    CANONICAL_VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA,
)
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.portfolio.vanilla_forward_curve import (
    CANONICAL_VANILLA_FORWARD_CURVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA,
)
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.portfolio.vanilla_forward_node import (
    CANONICAL_VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA,
)
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import MarketRiskIngestData
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import validate_column_lengths


def _get_data() -> MarketRiskIngestData:
    # Concat via DataFrame -> dict round-trip so column unions land
    # cleanly (per-fixture columns are a subset of the full schema).
    per_instrument_dfs = [
        pd.DataFrame(CANONICAL_SPOT_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA),
        pd.DataFrame(CANONICAL_VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA),
        pd.DataFrame(CANONICAL_VANILLA_FORWARD_CURVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA),
        pd.DataFrame(CANONICAL_VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA),
        pd.DataFrame(
            CANONICAL_VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA
        ),
    ]

    # Drop entirely-NA columns *before* concat so a missing column on
    # one input doesn't bleed object dtype into the others.
    valid_dfs = [
        df.dropna(axis=1, how="all") for df in per_instrument_dfs if not df.empty
    ]

    combined_df = (
        pd.concat(valid_dfs, ignore_index=True) if valid_dfs else pd.DataFrame()
    )

    return cast(MarketRiskIngestData, combined_df.to_dict(orient="list"))


CANONICAL_EXHAUSTIVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA: MarketRiskIngestData = _get_data()

validate_column_lengths(CANONICAL_EXHAUSTIVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA)


_CANONICAL_EXHAUSTIVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DF = pd.DataFrame(
    CANONICAL_EXHAUSTIVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA
)


def _debug_print() -> None:
    from riskquant.utils.printer import (
        print_calling_file,
        print_df,
        print_dict,
        print_success_message,
    )

    print_calling_file()

    n_rows = len(CANONICAL_EXHAUSTIVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA["uid"])
    title = f"Canonical Exhaustive Market-Risk Ingest Data ({n_rows} rows)"
    print_dict(CANONICAL_EXHAUSTIVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA, title=title)

    title += " (as DataFrame)"
    print_df(_CANONICAL_EXHAUSTIVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DF, title=title)

    print_success_message(add_space_before=False)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    _debug_print()
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/portfolio/spot.py
"""
Canonical SPOT ingest fixture.

11 rows / 11 unique uids. Exercises every resolution path the SPOT
compiler can take against the canonical static + proxy config universe:

Per-row coverage
----------------
  uid  underlying              kind                       notes
  ---  ------------------      -----------------------    ----------------------
   1   AAPL_EQ                 node, no FX                long
   2   AAPL_EQ                 node, no FX                long  (duplicate row
                                                                of uid 1 — same
                                                                underlying,
                                                                identical
                                                                resolution path)
   3   USDEUR                  node, FX direct hit        long
   4   USDEUR                  node, FX direct hit        short (settle ccy
                                                                forces inversion)
   5   USDGBP                  node, RF-LEVEL PROXY       proxies to USDEUR
                                                          scale 0.85
   6   EURCHF                  node, RF-LEVEL PROXY       proxies to USDEUR
                                                          scale 0.60
   7   BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]    curve-point, direct        long
   8   BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]    curve-point, direct        short, EUR settle
                                                          (exercises FX leg)
   9   WTI_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]      curve-point, CONTAINER     proxies to
                               PROXY                      BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]
                                                          scale 0.95
  10   HENRY_HUB_GAS_FWD[M+1]  curve-point, CONTAINER     proxies to
                               PROXY                      BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+1]
                                                          scale 1.40
  11   USD_LIBOR_3M[10Y]       curve-point, RF-LEVEL      proxies to
                               PROXY                      USD_SOFR_OIS[10Y]
                                                          scale 1.00

Netting-set linkage is exercised across uids 1-4 (three sets) so the
credit-exposure pipeline has something to chew on without forcing the
SPOT validator into any new branches.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import MarketRiskIngestData

    generate_netting_set_ids,
    validate_column_lengths,
)

    return {
        "uid": _UIDS,
        "instrument": [Instrument.SPOT.name] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "quantity": [
            100.0,  # uid 1.  long AAPL
            100.0,  # uid 2.  long AAPL (duplicate underlying)
            5_000_000.0,  # uid 3.  long USDEUR FX spot
            -5_000_000.0,  # uid 4.  short USDEUR (settle USD forces inversion)
            -3_000_000.0,  # uid 5.  short USDGBP (RF-proxy underlying)
            2_000_000.0,  # uid 6.  long  EURCHF (RF-proxy underlying)
            10_000.0,  # uid 7.  long  BRENT front-month
            -5_000.0,  # uid 8.  short BRENT front-month in EUR settle
            8_000.0,  # uid 9.  long  WTI  front-month (container-proxy)
            -2_500.0,  # uid 10. short HENRY_HUB [M+1] (container-proxy)
            1_000_000.0,  # uid 11. long  USD_LIBOR_3M[10Y] (RF-proxy)
        ],
        "underlying": [
            "AAPL_EQ",  # node, no FX
            "AAPL_EQ",  # node, no FX (dup)
            "USDEUR",  # node, direct FX
            "USDEUR",  # node, inverse direct FX
            "USDGBP",  # node, RF-proxy
            "EURCHF",  # node, RF-proxy
            "BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]",  # curve-point, direct
            "BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]",  # curve-point, direct (different settle)
            "WTI_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]",  # curve-point, container-proxy
            "HENRY_HUB_GAS_FWD[M+1]",  # curve-point, container-proxy
            "USD_LIBOR_3M[10Y]",  # curve-point, RF-proxy
        ],
        "settlement_ccy": [
            "USD",
            "EUR",
            "EUR",
            "USD",
            "GBP",
            "CHF",
            "USD",
            "EUR",  # forces FX leg over the BRENT[M+0] underlying
            "USD",
            "USD",
            "USD",
        ],
        # netting_set_id: small set used by exposure pipeline. None -> no
        # netting linkage.
        "netting_set_id": generate_netting_set_ids(n=_NUM_UIDS, none_probability=0.5),
        # SPOT-irrelevant columns: all None.
        "strike_price": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "strike_ccy": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "is_call": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "expiry_date": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "delivery_date": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "settlement_date": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "under_discount_curve": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "strike_discount_curve": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
    }


CANONICAL_SPOT_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA: MarketRiskIngestData = _get_data()
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/portfolio/vanilla_european_option_curve.py
"""
Canonical VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE ingest fixture.

VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE = European option on a forward curve.
Underlying is a curve container; vol is sourced from a surface container;
delivery + expiry + strike + is_call + vol-container all live.

Coverage:
  - Call on commodity curve, no FX             -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  vol surface
                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF (uid=400)
  - Put  on commodity curve, no FX             -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF (uid=401)
  - Call on commodity curve, FX settlement     -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  EUR settlement
                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF (uid=402)
  - Call on commodity curve, CONTAINER PROXY   -> WTI_CRUDE_FWD ->
                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_FWD, scale 0.95;

                                                  resolved via the underlying-
                                                  container proxy on the vol
                                                  side at the runtime builder.
                                                  Here we point at
                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF
                                                  directly. (uid=403)
  - Put  on FX curve via surface (uid=404)     -> USDEUR; vol surface
                                                  USDEUR_VOL_SURF is
                                                  declared DELTA_CALL — the
                                                  runtime market-data builder
                                                  currently rejects DELTA
                                                  surfaces, so this trade

                                                  See notes/REFACTORS_NEEDED.md.

            "USD",

        ],
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/portfolio/vanilla_european_option_node.py
"""
Canonical VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE ingest fixture.

VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE = European option whose underlying resolves
to a single risk-factor node (or curve-point relative name). Vol is
sourced from a vol container — conceptually a vol-NODE, but the
canonical static config does not currently declare any vol-node
containers; only vol surfaces exist. This fixture therefore points
`volatility_container_name` at the parent vol SURFACE container, which
ingests cleanly (the schema accepts any container name) but exposes the
documented option-node-vol-source architectural gap: the kernel reads
`vol_nodes[v_uix]` and the slot reserved for the surface id will not
have a populated scalar-vol row at runtime.

The new hoisted `if vol_uix >= 0:` sentinel branch in the kernel makes
this SAFE (no out-of-bounds UB) but not CORRECT — see
notes/REFACTORS_NEEDED.md for the three follow-up options.

Coverage (resolution paths, not end-to-end PV):
  - Call on equity node, no FX                 -> AAPL_EQ      (uid=300)
                                                  vol AAPL_VOL_SURF
  - Put  on equity node, no FX                 -> AAPL_EQ      (uid=301)
                                                  vol AAPL_VOL_SURF
  - Call on FX node, settlement in foreign ccy -> USDEUR       (uid=302)
                                                  strike=EUR, settle=EUR
                                                  vol USDEUR_VOL_SURF
                                                  (DELTA_CALL — runtime
                                                  builder rejects today)
  - Put  on FX node, RF-LEVEL PROXY underlying -> USDGBP       (uid=303)
                                                  proxies to USDEUR
                                                  vol USDEUR_VOL_SURF
                                                  (DELTA_CALL — same)
  - Call on curve-point pinned underlying      -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]
                                                  (uid=304)
                                                  vol BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF
  - Put  on curve-point, CONTAINER PROXY       -> WTI_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]
                                                  proxies to BRENT[M+0]
                                                  (uid=305)
                                                  vol BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import MarketRiskIngestData
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import (
    generate_netting_set_ids,
    validate_column_lengths,
)
from riskquant.portfolio.instruments import Instrument


def _get_data() -> MarketRiskIngestData:
    return {
        "uid": [300, 301, 302, 303, 304, 305],
        "instrument": [Instrument.VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE.name] * 6,
        "quantity": [
            100.0,  # 300. long call AAPL
            -100.0,  # 301. short put AAPL
            500_000.0,  # 302. long call USDEUR
            -250_000.0,  # 303. short put USDGBP (RF-proxy)
            1_000.0,  # 304. long call BRENT[M+0]
            -500.0,  # 305. short put WTI[M+0] (container-proxy)
        ],
        "underlying": [
            "AAPL_EQ",
            "AAPL_EQ",
            "USDEUR",
            "USDGBP",
            "BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]",
            "WTI_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]",
        ],
        "settlement_ccy": [
            "USD",
            "USD",
            "EUR",
            "GBP",
            "USD",
            "USD",
        ],
        "strike_price": [180.0, 175.0, 1.0850, 1.2750, 78.0, 75.0],
        "strike_ccy": [
            "USD",
            "USD",
            "EUR",
            "GBP",
            "USD",
            "USD",
        ],
        # is_call: 1.0 = call, 0.0 = put. Mixed to exercise both legs of
        # the branchless cp_mult encoding.
        "is_call": [1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0],
        "expiry_date": [
            "2026-06-19",
            "2026-09-18",
            "2026-12-18",
            "2027-03-19",
            "2026-06-26",
            "2026-09-25",
        ],
        "settlement_date": [
            "2026-06-22",
            "2026-09-21",
            "2026-12-21",
            "2027-03-22",
            "2026-06-29",
            "2026-09-28",
        ],
        "under_discount_curve": [
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
        ],
        "strike_discount_curve": [
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "EUR_ESTR_OIS",
            "GBP_SONIA_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
        ],
        # OPTION_NODE has no delivery (single rf underlying).
        "delivery_date": [None] * 6,
        # Vol-container pointer. The option-node kernel reads
        # `vol_nodes[uix]` — a separate axis from vol_surfaces — and the
        # canonical static config has no vol-node entries. Until a vol-
        # node universe lands (or the option-node kernel is refactored
        # to source from `vol_surfaces`), this fixture points at the
        # parent vol surface so ingest validation passes; the runtime
        # PV will be zero or garbage at the surface's universe slot.
        # See notes/REFACTORS_NEEDED.md.
        "volatility_container_name": [
            "AAPL_VOL_SURF",
            "AAPL_VOL_SURF",
            "USDEUR_VOL_SURF",
            "USDEUR_VOL_SURF",  # USDGBP proxies to USDEUR
            "BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF",
            "BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF",  # WTI proxies to BRENT
        ],
        "netting_set_id": generate_netting_set_ids(n=6, none_probability=0.0),
    }


CANONICAL_VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA: MarketRiskIngestData = _get_data()

validate_column_lengths(
    CANONICAL_VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA
)


_CANONICAL_VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DF = pd.DataFrame(
    CANONICAL_VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA
)


def _debug_print() -> None:
    from riskquant.utils.printer import (
        print_calling_file,
        print_df,
        print_dict,
        print_success_message,
    )

    print_calling_file()

    print("Canonical VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE Market-Risk Ingest Data:")
    print_dict(CANONICAL_VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA)

    print("As DataFrame:")
    print_df(_CANONICAL_VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DF)

    print_success_message(add_space_before=False)
```

```python
"""
Canonical VANILLA_FORWARD_CURVE ingest fixture.

VANILLA_FORWARD_CURVE = forward whose underlying is a *curve container*

the curve's roll rule. Strike + delivery + settlement + discounting are
all live.

Coverage:
  - Curve underlying, no FX, no discount       -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  (uid=200)
  - Curve underlying, no FX, with discount     -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  + USD_SOFR_OIS
                                                  (uid=201)
  - Curve underlying, FX, cross-curve discount -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  EUR settlement,
                                                  USD_SOFR_OIS / EUR_ESTR_OIS
                                                  (uid=202)

                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_FWD,
                                                  scale 0.95
                                                  (uid=203)

                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_FWD,
                                                  EUR settlement,

                                                  discounted via

                                                  non-default discount
                                                  basis (uid=205)

  POWER_CH_BASELOAD_HY -> CO2_EUA_ANNUAL_FWD
```

```python
                                                   discount via USD_SOFR_OIS
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/schemas.py
"""
Canonical TypedDict schemas for every human-facing ingest frame.

Schemas mirror the SoA contracts they feed:
- Market-risk frames carry trade-keyed columns + optional credit linkage.
- JTD frames carry obligor-keyed *model-specific* columns ONLY. PD and
  LGD live on the obligor master; they are joined onto JTD rows at
  compile time and are never duplicated on per-model frames.
- Obligor- and netting-set-master frames are first-class schemas, one
  per master.
"""

from collections.abc import Sequence
from typing import TypedDict

######################
# RISK STATIC CONFIG #
######################


# ============================================================
# CURRENCY
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigCcy(TypedDict):
    ccy_name: Sequence[str]
    ccy_id: Sequence[int]


# ============================================================
# HOLIDAY CALENDAR
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigHolidayCalendar(TypedDict):
    holiday_calendar_name: Sequence[str]
    holiday_date: Sequence[str]  # ISO yyyy-mm-dd; compiler converts to int32 ordinals


# ============================================================
# SPOT ROLL RULE
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigSpotRollRule(TypedDict):
    rule_name: Sequence[str]
    roll_method: Sequence[str]
    offset_in_tenor_units: Sequence[int]
    roll_month: Sequence[int | None]
    roll_day: Sequence[int | None]
    fh_bh_mmdd: Sequence[str | None]
    post_roll_shift: Sequence[int]


# ============================================================
# NODE  (scalar; IS its own risk factor)
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigNodeMeta(TypedDict):
    node_id: Sequence[int]
    node_name: Sequence[str]
    quote_ccy: Sequence[str]
    asset_class: Sequence[str]
    asset_subclass: Sequence[str]
    unit_type: Sequence[str]
    unit_scale: Sequence[str]
    shift_type: Sequence[str]
    fx_base_ccy: Sequence[str | None]


# ============================================================
# CURVE
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigCurveMeta(TypedDict):
    curve_id: Sequence[int]
    curve_name: Sequence[str]
    quote_ccy: Sequence[str]
    asset_class: Sequence[str]
    asset_subclass: Sequence[str]
    unit_type: Sequence[str]
    unit_scale: Sequence[str]
    shift_type: Sequence[str]
    x_axis_kind: Sequence[str]
    x_interp_method: Sequence[str]
    x_extrap_method: Sequence[str]
    spot_roll_rule_name: Sequence[str | None]
    discount_basis: Sequence[str]
    is_default_discount_for_ccy: Sequence[bool]


class RiskStaticConfigCurvePointMeta(TypedDict):
    rf_id: Sequence[int]
    curve_id: Sequence[int]
    tenor_token: Sequence[str]


# ============================================================
# SURFACE
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigSurfaceMeta(TypedDict):
    surface_id: Sequence[int]
    surface_name: Sequence[str]
    quote_ccy: Sequence[str]
    asset_class: Sequence[str]
    asset_subclass: Sequence[str]
    unit_type: Sequence[str]
    unit_scale: Sequence[str]
    shift_type: Sequence[str]
    x_axis_kind: Sequence[str]
    x_interp_method: Sequence[str]
    x_extrap_method: Sequence[str]
    spot_roll_rule_name: Sequence[str | None]
    y_axis_kind: Sequence[str]
    y_interp_method: Sequence[str]
    y_extrap_method: Sequence[str]


class RiskStaticConfigSurfacePointMeta(TypedDict):
    rf_id: Sequence[int]
    surface_id: Sequence[int]
    x_tenor_token: Sequence[str]
    y_label: Sequence[str]


# ============================================================
# TOP-LEVEL
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigData(TypedDict):
    ccy: RiskStaticConfigCcy
    holiday_calendars: RiskStaticConfigHolidayCalendar
    spot_roll_rule: RiskStaticConfigSpotRollRule
    node_meta: RiskStaticConfigNodeMeta
    curve_meta: RiskStaticConfigCurveMeta
    curve_point_meta: RiskStaticConfigCurvePointMeta
    surface_meta: RiskStaticConfigSurfaceMeta
    surface_point_meta: RiskStaticConfigSurfacePointMeta


#####################################
# END OF RISK STATIC CONFIG SCHEMAS #
#####################################

#####################
# RISK PROXY CONFIG #
#####################


class RiskProxyConfigData(TypedDict):
    proxying_level: Sequence[str]  # "CONTAINER" | "RISK_FACTOR"
    required_name: Sequence[str]
    proxy_name: Sequence[str]
    vol_scaling_factor: Sequence[float]


# ============================================================
# BASE
# ============================================================


class _BaseIngestData(TypedDict):
    uid: Sequence[int]
    instrument: Sequence[str]


# ============================================================
# MARKET RISK
# ============================================================


class _MarketCoreIngestData(_BaseIngestData):
    quantity: Sequence[float]
    underlying: Sequence[str]
    settlement_ccy: Sequence[str]


class _OptionalNettingSet(TypedDict, total=False):
    # Effective-netting-set linkage. Optional: absent/None -> NO_NETTING_SET.
    netting_set_id: Sequence[int | None]


class MarketRiskIngestData(_MarketCoreIngestData, _OptionalNettingSet):
    strike_price: Sequence[float | None]
    strike_ccy: Sequence[str | None]

    is_call: Sequence[float | None]

    expiry_date: Sequence[str | None]
    delivery_date: Sequence[str | None]
    settlement_date: Sequence[str | None]

    under_discount_curve: Sequence[str | None]
    strike_discount_curve: Sequence[str | None]

    # Name of the volatility container (vol surface for option-curve;
    # vol surface or vol-node container for option-node) the option's
    # implied vol is sourced from. Required for OPTION-family rows,
    # None for SPOT and FORWARD rows. Resolved to an int32 container
    # id at compile time against `static_config.container_name_to_id`.
    volatility_container_name: Sequence[str | None]


# ============================================================
# CREDIT MASTERS
# ============================================================


class _OptionalObligorName(TypedDict, total=False):
    # Effective-netting-set linkage. Optional: absent/None -> NO_NETTING_SET.
    name: Sequence[str | None]


class ObligorCreditIngestData(_OptionalObligorName):
    """
    Obligor master ingest frame.

    Single source of truth for obligor-level credit attributes. PD and
    LGD live here, not on per-model JTD frames — that's the duplication
    the master is designed to prevent.

    `parent_uid` is nullable: NA/None marks an ultimate obligor and is
    mapped to `NO_PARENT_OBLIGOR` at compile time. The rating/sector/
    country int codes are placeholders for IntEnum taxonomies wired in a
    follow-up; `NO_RATING_ID`, `NO_SECTOR_ID`, `NO_COUNTRY_ID` mark
    unknowns.
    """

    uid: Sequence[int]
    pd: Sequence[float]
    lgd: Sequence[float]

    rating_id: Sequence[int]  # int8 enum, source: config/enums/credit_ratings (TBD)
    sector_id: Sequence[int]  # int32 enum (TBD)
    country_id: Sequence[int]  # int32 enum (TBD)

    parent_uid: Sequence[int | None]  # None -> NO_PARENT_OBLIGOR


class NettingSetMasterIngestData(TypedDict):
    """
    Netting-set master ingest frame.

    Carries the netting-set identifier (joined to the portfolio's
    `netting_set_id` column), the owning obligor, and net collateral
    (haircut-adjusted, >= 0).
    """

    uid: Sequence[int]
    obligor_uid: Sequence[int]
    net_collateral: Sequence[float]


# ============================================================
# CREDIT RISK | DETERMINISTIC JTD
# ============================================================


class DeterministicJTDIngestData(_BaseIngestData):
    """
    Deterministic JTD model frame.

    Carries only the dispatch keys (`uid`, `instrument`). LGD comes from
    the obligor master; PD is not read by the deterministic kernel; EAD
    flows in per scenario from the exposure engine. The frame is
    deliberately minimal — anything else here would duplicate state that
    already has a home elsewhere.
    """


# ============================================================
# CREDIT RISK | COPULA JTD (single-factor copulas)
# ============================================================


class _SingleFactorCopulaJTDCoreIngestData(_BaseIngestData):
    """
    Single-factor copula JTD core: model-specific fields only.

    PD and LGD are joined off the obligor master at compile time and
    intentionally absent from this schema.
    """

    beta: Sequence[float]
    systemic_factor: Sequence[str]
    idiosyncratic_factor: Sequence[str]


class GaussianCopulaJTDIngestData(_SingleFactorCopulaJTDCoreIngestData):
    pass


class TCopulaJTDIngestData(_SingleFactorCopulaJTDCoreIngestData):
    chi_squared_factor: Sequence[str]
    nu: Sequence[float]
```
</file>

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\ingest_fixtures\utils.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/utils.py


from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Any, cast

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import (
    DeterministicJTDIngestData,
    GaussianCopulaJTDIngestData,
    MarketRiskIngestData,
    NettingSetMasterIngestData,
    ObligorCreditIngestData,
    RiskProxyConfigData,
    RiskStaticConfigCcy,
    RiskStaticConfigCurveMeta,
    RiskStaticConfigCurvePointMeta,
    RiskStaticConfigData,
    RiskStaticConfigHolidayCalendar,
    RiskStaticConfigNodeMeta,
    RiskStaticConfigSpotRollRule,
    RiskStaticConfigSurfaceMeta,
    RiskStaticConfigSurfacePointMeta,
    TCopulaJTDIngestData,
)

_DATA = (
    MarketRiskIngestData
    | GaussianCopulaJTDIngestData
    | TCopulaJTDIngestData
    | DeterministicJTDIngestData
    | ObligorCreditIngestData
    | NettingSetMasterIngestData
    # Risk Static Config
    | RiskStaticConfigSpotRollRule
    | RiskStaticConfigData
    | RiskStaticConfigCcy
    | RiskStaticConfigHolidayCalendar
    | RiskStaticConfigNodeMeta
    | RiskStaticConfigCurveMeta
    | RiskStaticConfigCurvePointMeta
    | RiskStaticConfigSurfaceMeta
    | RiskStaticConfigSurfacePointMeta
    # Risk Proxy Config
    | RiskProxyConfigData
)


def _validate_single_table(data: Mapping[str, Sequence[Any]]) -> None:
    lengths = {len(v) for v in data.values()}

    if len(lengths) > 1:
        raise ValueError(f"Inconsistent column lengths: {lengths}")


def validate_column_lengths(data: Any) -> None:
    """
    Validates:
    - flat columnar dicts: Dict[str, Sequence]
    - nested config: Dict[str, Dict[str, Sequence]]
    """

    # Case 1: Nested (RiskStaticConfigData)
    if all(isinstance(v, Mapping) for v in data.values()):
        for k, sub_table in data.items():
            if not isinstance(sub_table, Mapping):
                raise TypeError(f"{k} is not a valid table")

            _validate_single_table(cast(Mapping[str, Sequence[Any]], sub_table))
        return

    # Case 2: Flat table
    _validate_single_table(cast(Mapping[str, Sequence[Any]], data))


def generate_netting_set_ids(
    *,
    n: int,
    none_probability: float = 0.2,
) -> list[int | None]:

    import numpy as np

    from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.netting_set.minimal_netting_set_master import (
        CANONICAL_NETTING_SET_UIDS,
    )
    from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.rng import get_canonical_rng

    _rng: np.random.Generator = get_canonical_rng()

    # 1. Generate the remaining IDs from valid options
    ns_ids = _rng.choice(
        a=CANONICAL_NETTING_SET_UIDS,
        size=n,
        replace=True,
    ).tolist()

    # 2. Randomly assign None based on the specified probability
    none_mask = _rng.random(size=n) < none_probability
    for i in range(n):
        if none_mask[i]:
            ns_ids[i] = None

    # 3. Combine and shuffle (shuffle works in-place)
    _rng.shuffle(ns_ids)

    return ns_ids
```
</file>

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\portfolio\blocks\base_block.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/portfolio/blocks/base_block.py

"""
Base class for all Struct-of-Arrays (SoA) trade blocks.

`Block` carries only what is universal to every compiled block: the flat int64
`trade_uids` array and the `INSTRUMENT_ID` dispatch tag. All economic SoA arrays
live on the concrete subclasses.

`INSTRUMENT_ID` is the single integer the valuation engine dispatches on: it
indexes directly into `BLOCK_EVALUATORS`, so no `type()` calls, no isinstance
ladders, no string keys. The base value is the `_UNSET` sentinel; every
concrete block MUST override it with its `Instrument` enum id. The override is
enforced at class-creation time by `__init_subclass__`, which fails loudly the
moment a new block type is added without wiring its id — a compile-time guard
rather than a runtime surprise.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, fields
from typing import Any, ClassVar

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt

# Sentinel for "concrete subclass forgot to set INSTRUMENT_ID". Negative so it
# can never collide with a real Instrument enum id or index a dispatch table.
_UNSET_INSTRUMENT_ID: int = -999


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class Block:
    """Universal base for compiled SoA trade blocks."""

    # Dispatch tag. ClassVar => not a dataclass field, zero per-instance cost.
    INSTRUMENT_ID: ClassVar[int] = _UNSET_INSTRUMENT_ID

    trade_uids: npt.NDArray[np.int64]

    def __init_subclass__(cls, **kwargs: object) -> None:
        """
        Enforce the dispatch contract for *concrete* block subclasses.

        Intermediate abstract bases (names starting with '_') are exempt: they
        legitimately leave `INSTRUMENT_ID` unset for their own subclasses to
        define. Any concrete block that ships without an id is a wiring bug and
        is rejected here, at import time.
        """
        # super().__init_subclass__(**kwargs)
        is_abstract_base = cls.__name__.startswith("_")
        if not is_abstract_base and cls.INSTRUMENT_ID == _UNSET_INSTRUMENT_ID:
            raise TypeError(
                f"Block subclass '{cls.__name__}' must set INSTRUMENT_ID "
                f"(its riskquant.portfolio.instruments id) for engine dispatch."
            )

    @property
    def num_trades(self) -> int:
        """Number of trades packed into this block."""
        return self.trade_uids.shape[0]

    def print_block(
        self,
        *,
        max_items: int | None = 10,
        float_precision: int = 4,
        sep: str = ", ",
        pad: int = 15,  # Increased default pad slightly to account for the new '_' characters
        thousand_sep: str = "_",
    ) -> None:
        """Print every SoA field with shape, dtype, and a value preview."""

        print(f"{type(self).__name__}:")
        for f in fields(self):
            v = getattr(self, f.name)
            if isinstance(v, np.ndarray):
                # Replace lambdas with typed inner functions to satisfy Pyright/MyPy
                def fmt_float(x: Any) -> str:
                    return f"{x:>{pad}{thousand_sep}.{float_precision}f}"

                def fmt_int(x: Any) -> str:
                    # Use 'd' (decimal integer) instead of a hacked float format
                    return f"{x:>{pad}{thousand_sep}d}"

                # Format the array preview cleanly
                val_preview = np.array2string(
                    v[:max_items],
                    separator=sep,
                    max_line_width=100_000,
                    formatter={
                        "float_kind": fmt_float,
                        "int_kind": fmt_int,
                    },
                )

                print(
                    f"  {f.name:<33} | shape: {v.shape!s:>10} | "
                    f"dtype: {v.dtype!s:>10} | values: {val_preview}"
                )
            else:
                print(f"  {f.name:<33} {v}")
        print()
```
</file>

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\portfolio\blocks\spot_block.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/portfolio/blocks/spot_block.py
"""
Compiled, memory-efficient container for spot positions.

`SpotBlock` is the struct-of-arrays representation of a set of spot trades.
The block is intentionally compact (NumPy arrays, primitive numeric dtypes)
so it can be held efficiently in memory and passed between processes in
high-throughput valuation workflows.

Architectural contract
----------------------
* Row `i` of every array corresponds to the trade with UID `trade_uids[i]`.
* Every `_uix` field is a zero-based, contiguous index produced by the
  portfolio engine's `UixMapper` — NOT a global static-config ID. The block
  carries execution coordinates only.
* `fx_spots_effective_uix` indexes into `MarketDataMatrix.fx_spots_effective`,
  the deduplicated effective FX-transform matrix. No invert flag is carried;
  the inversion (if any) has been folded into the effective matrix at the
  builder edge. Pricer kernels do one unconditional gather + multiply.
* `INVALID_UIX (< 0)` in any `_uix` field signals "no factor required for
  this trade" and is the only branch the pricer kernel performs (hoisted
  outside the scenario loop).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import ClassVar

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt

from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.base_block import Block
from riskquant.portfolio.instruments import SPOT_ID


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class SpotBlock(Block):
    """Struct-of-arrays for compiled spot trades."""

    INSTRUMENT_ID: ClassVar[int] = int(SPOT_ID)

    # ========== TRADE IDENTITY ==========
    # Unique trade identifiers, one per row.
    trade_uids: npt.NDArray[np.int64]

    # ========== ECONOMIC TERMS ==========
    # Signed quantities / notionals for the spot positions.
    qty: npt.NDArray[np.float64]

    # ========== MARKET LINKAGES (EXECUTION UIX) ==========
    # Index into `MarketDataMatrix.prices` for this row.
    price_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]

    # Index into `MarketDataMatrix.fx_spots_effective`, or INVALID_UIX
    # (< 0) when no FX conversion is required for this trade.
    fx_spots_effective_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]
```
</file>

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\portfolio\blocks\vanilla_european_option_block.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/portfolio/blocks/vanilla_european_option_block.py
"""
Compiled blocks for vanilla European option trades.

Architectural contract
----------------------
* Row `i` of every array corresponds to the trade with UID `trade_uids[i]`.
* Every `_uix` field is a zero-based, contiguous execution index produced by
  the portfolio engine's `UixMapper` — NOT a global static-config ID.
* FX legs are carried as `fx_*_to_settle_effective_uix` indexing
  `MarketDataMatrix.fx_spots_effective`. No invert flags survive into the
  block layer.
* `cp_mult` packs `is_call` into `+1` / `-1` for branchless Black-76 payoff.
* `INVALID_UIX (< 0)` in any optional `_uix` field signals "no factor
  required for this trade" and is the only branch the pricer kernel
  performs (hoisted outside the scenario loop).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import ClassVar

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt

from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.base_block import Block
from riskquant.portfolio.instruments import (
    VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE_ID,
    VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE_ID,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class _VanillaEuropeanOptionBaseBlock(Block):
    """Common SoA fields shared by all vanilla European option blocks."""

    # ========== TRADE IDENTITY ==========
    trade_uids: npt.NDArray[np.int64]

    # ========== ECONOMIC TERMS ==========
    qty: npt.NDArray[np.float64]
    strike: npt.NDArray[np.float64]
    # +1 for call, -1 for put, precomputed for branchless payoff sign.
    cp_mult: npt.NDArray[np.int8]

    # ========== DATES (DATE UIX) ==========
    # Indices into `unique_dates_ord32`.
    expiry_date_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]
    settlement_date_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]

    # ========== FX LINKAGES (EFFECTIVE UIX) ==========
    # Indices into `MarketDataMatrix.fx_spots_effective`. INVALID_UIX (< 0)
    # signals "no FX conversion required" for that leg.
    fx_under_to_settle_effective_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]
    fx_strike_to_settle_effective_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]

    # ========== DISCOUNT LINKAGES (CURVE UIX) ==========
    # Indices into `MarketDataMatrix.discount_factor_grid` axis 0.
    # INVALID_UIX (< 0) when no discount applies on that leg.
    under_discount_curve_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]
    strike_discount_curve_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock(_VanillaEuropeanOptionBaseBlock):
    """European option whose underlying is sourced from a scalar node."""

    INSTRUMENT_ID: ClassVar[int] = int(VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE_ID)

    # ========== MARKET LINKAGES ==========
    # Index into `MarketDataMatrix.forward_nodes`.
    underlying_price_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]
    # Index into `MarketDataMatrix.vol_nodes`.
    vol_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock(_VanillaEuropeanOptionBaseBlock):
    """European option whose underlying and vol come from curves/surfaces."""

    INSTRUMENT_ID: ClassVar[int] = int(VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE_ID)

    # ========== DELIVERY DATE (DATE UIX) ==========
    delivery_date_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]

    # ========== MARKET LINKAGES ==========
    # Index into `MarketDataMatrix.price_curve_grid` axis 0.
    underlying_price_curve_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]
    # Index into `MarketDataMatrix.vol_surfaces` axis 0.
    vol_surface_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]

    # Y-axis kind for the vol surface (e.g., strike, log-moneyness).
    vol_surface_y_axis_type_id: npt.NDArray[np.int8]
```

```python
# src/riskquant/portfolio/blocks/vanilla_forward_block.py
"""
Compiled blocks for vanilla forward trades.

Architectural contract
----------------------
* Row `i` of every array corresponds to the trade with UID `trade_uids[i]`.
* Every `_uix` field is a zero-based, contiguous index produced by the
  portfolio engine's `UixMapper` — NOT a global static-config ID.
* FX legs are carried as `fx_*_to_settle_effective_uix` indexing the
  deduplicated `MarketDataMatrix.fx_spots_effective` matrix. No invert
  flags survive into the block layer — inversion is folded into the
  effective matrix at the builder edge.
* `INVALID_UIX (< 0)` in any optional `_uix` field signals "no factor
  required for this trade" and is the only branch the pricer kernel
  performs (hoisted outside the scenario loop).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import ClassVar

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt

from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.base_block import Block
from riskquant.portfolio.instruments import (
    VANILLA_FORWARD_CURVE_ID,
    VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE_ID,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class _VanillaForwardBaseBlock(Block):
    """Common SoA fields shared by all vanilla forward blocks."""

    # ========== TRADE IDENTITY ==========
    trade_uids: npt.NDArray[np.int64]

    # ========== ECONOMIC TERMS ==========
    qty: npt.NDArray[np.float64]
    strike: npt.NDArray[np.float64]

    # ========== SETTLEMENT DATE (DATE UIX) ==========
    # Index into `unique_dates_ord32` for cashflow settlement.
    settlement_date_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]

    # ========== FX LINKAGES (EFFECTIVE UIX) ==========
    # Index into `MarketDataMatrix.fx_spots_effective` for the under->settle
    # transform. INVALID_UIX (< 0) when no FX needed for this leg.
    fx_under_to_settle_effective_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]

    # Index into `MarketDataMatrix.fx_spots_effective` for the strike->settle
    # transform. INVALID_UIX (< 0) when no FX needed for this leg.
    fx_strike_to_settle_effective_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]

    # ========== DISCOUNT LINKAGES (CURVE UIX) ==========
    # Indices into `MarketDataMatrix.discount_factor_grid` axis 0.
    # INVALID_UIX (< 0) when no discount applies on that leg.
    under_discount_curve_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]
    strike_discount_curve_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class VanillaForwardNodeBlock(_VanillaForwardBaseBlock):
    """Forward priced directly off a scalar node."""

    INSTRUMENT_ID: ClassVar[int] = int(VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE_ID)

    # ========== UNDERLYING MARKET LINKAGE ==========
    # Index into `MarketDataMatrix.forward_nodes` axis 0.
    price_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class VanillaForwardCurveBlock(_VanillaForwardBaseBlock):
    """Forward priced off a curve requiring delivery-date interpolation."""

    INSTRUMENT_ID: ClassVar[int] = int(VANILLA_FORWARD_CURVE_ID)

    # ========== DELIVERY DATE (DATE UIX) ==========
    delivery_date_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]

    # ========== UNDERLYING MARKET LINKAGE ==========
    # Index into `MarketDataMatrix.price_curve_grid` axis 0.
    price_curve_uix: npt.NDArray[np.int32]
```

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\portfolio\compiled.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/portfolio/compiled.py

"""
Compiled portfolio container.

`CompiledPortfolio` is a pure portfolio-layer artifact: the frozen, valuation-
ready output of block compilation. It holds compiled SoA blocks plus the
metadata the engine needs to walk them — and nothing from the valuation layer.

Layering
--------
This module imports only `portfolio.blocks`. It deliberately does NOT import
pricers, risk evaluators, or the `Evaluator` protocol. Evaluator resolution is
the valuation engine's responsibility: it dispatches on each block's
`INSTRUMENT_ID` class constant via its own `BLOCK_EVALUATORS` table.

The dependency therefore points one way only:

    valuation  ──▶  portfolio        (allowed)
    portfolio  ──▶  valuation        (never)

The previous design bound pricer callables into `_evaluators` inside
`__post_init__`, which made a data container reach up into the valuation layer
and created the `compiled <-> valuation.engine` import cycle. Removing those
references eliminates the cycle by construction — there is no edge left to
break, deferred-import trick or otherwise.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Iterator
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import TypeAlias

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt

from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.spot_block import SpotBlock
from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.vanilla_european_option_block import (
    VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock,
    VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock,
)
from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.vanilla_forward_block import (
    VanillaForwardCurveBlock,
    VanillaForwardNodeBlock,
)

BlockT: TypeAlias = (
    # --- Market Risk ---
    SpotBlock
    | VanillaForwardNodeBlock
    | VanillaForwardCurveBlock
    | VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock
    | VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class CompiledPortfolio:
    """
    Valuation-ready portfolio: an ordered set of compiled SoA blocks.

    Each populated block contributes one contiguous row-band to the eventual
    (n_trades x n_scenarios) valuation matrix; `block_offsets[i]` is the first
    row of block `i`. `trade_uids` aggregates every block's uids into one flat
    int64 array in the same order, so a portfolio-row index maps straight to a
    trade uid without per-block bookkeeping.
    """

    # Unique dates across the portfolio, as ordinals truncated to int32 for
    # engine-friendly storage and fast comparisons. Sorted ascending, unique.
    unique_dates_ord32: npt.NDArray[np.int32] = field(
        default_factory=lambda: np.empty(0, dtype=np.int32)
    )

    # Instrument blocks (SoA arrays); None blocks are compacted in __post_init__.
    spot: SpotBlock | None = None
    vanilla_forward_node: VanillaForwardNodeBlock | None = None
    vanilla_forward_curve: VanillaForwardCurveBlock | None = None
    vanilla_european_option_node: VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock | None = None
    vanilla_european_option_curve: VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock | None = None

    # Derived, set in __post_init__.
    _blocks: tuple[BlockT, ...] = field(init=False, repr=False)
    _block_offsets: npt.NDArray[np.int64] = field(init=False, repr=False)
    _total_blocks: int = field(init=False, repr=False)
    _total_trades: int = field(init=False, repr=False)
    _trade_uids: npt.NDArray[np.int64] = field(init=False, repr=False)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        # Construction-ordered slots; None slots are compacted out below. This
        # tuple order defines block ordering and therefore output-matrix bands.
        ordered: tuple[BlockT | None, ...] = (
            self.spot,
            self.vanilla_forward_node,
            self.vanilla_forward_curve,
            self.vanilla_european_option_node,
            self.vanilla_european_option_curve,
        )
        blocks: tuple[BlockT, ...] = tuple(b for b in ordered if b is not None)
        total_blocks: int = len(blocks)

        # Offsets via exclusive prefix-sum of per-block trade counts.
        block_offsets = np.zeros(total_blocks, dtype=np.int64)
        trade_counts = np.fromiter(
            (b.num_trades for b in blocks), dtype=np.int64, count=total_blocks
        )
        total_trades = int(trade_counts.sum())
        if total_blocks > 1:
            np.cumsum(trade_counts[:-1], out=block_offsets[1:])

        # Aggregate every block's uids into one flat array in portfolio order.
        trade_uids = np.empty(total_trades, dtype=np.int64)
        offset = 0
        for block in blocks:
            n = block.num_trades
            trade_uids[offset : offset + n] = block.trade_uids
            offset += n

        object.__setattr__(self, "_blocks", blocks)
        object.__setattr__(self, "_block_offsets", block_offsets)
        object.__setattr__(self, "_total_blocks", total_blocks)
        object.__setattr__(self, "_total_trades", total_trades)
        object.__setattr__(self, "_trade_uids", trade_uids)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    # Read-only accessors
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

    @property
    def blocks(self) -> tuple[BlockT, ...]:
        return self._blocks

    @property
    def block_offsets(self) -> npt.NDArray[np.int64]:
        return self._block_offsets

    @property
    def total_blocks(self) -> int:
        return self._total_blocks

    @property
    def total_trades(self) -> int:
        return self._total_trades

    @property
    def trade_uids(self) -> npt.NDArray[np.int64]:
        return self._trade_uids

    def iter_blocks(self) -> Iterator[BlockT]:
        yield from self._blocks

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    # Diagnostics
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

    def summary(self) -> None:
        """Print a console-friendly overview of block layout and trade counts."""
        print("=" * 90)
        print(f"{'COMPILED PORTFOLIO SUMMARY':^90}")
        print("=" * 90)
        print(f"Total Trades: {self._total_trades:,}")
        print(f"Total Blocks: {self._total_blocks}")
        print(f"Unique Dates: {len(self.unique_dates_ord32)}")
        print("-" * 90)
        print(f"{'Block Type':<32} | {'Trades':<10} | {'Offset'}")
        print("-" * 90)

        if self._total_blocks == 0:
            print(f"{'Empty Portfolio':^90}")
        else:
            for i, block in enumerate(self._blocks):
                print(
                    f"{type(block).__name__:<32} | "
                    f"{block.num_trades:<10,} | {self._block_offsets[i]}"
                )
        print("=" * 90 + "\n")

    def print_block_details(
        self,
        *,
        max_items: int | None = 10,
        float_precision: int = 0,
        sep: str = ", ",
        pad: int = 12,
        thousand_sep: str = "_",
    ) -> None:
        """Cascade into each block and print its raw SoA arrays."""
        print("=" * 90)
        print(f"{f'BLOCK DETAILS (MAX {max_items} ITEMS PER BLOCK)':^90}")
        print("=" * 90 + "\n")
        for block in self._blocks:
            title = f"Block: {type(block).__name__}"
            print("-" * len(title))
            print(title)
            print("-" * len(title))
            block.print_block(
                max_items=max_items,
                float_precision=float_precision,
                sep=sep,
                pad=pad,
                thousand_sep=thousand_sep,
            )
        print("=" * 90 + "\n")
```
</file>

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\portfolio\compilers\block_compilers\spot.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/portfolio/compilers/instruments/spot.py
"""
Spot block compiler.

Translates the spot-instrument sub-range of an `IngestBatch` (global IDs) into
a `SpotBlock` (execution UIXs) via the run's `UixMapper`. The only fields
that need translation are:
  - `price_uix`            : underlying_ids        -> market_id_universe
  - `fx_spots_effective_uix`: (fx_u2s_ids, fx_u2s_inv) -> fx_effective_pairs

`qty` and `trade_uids` are pure economics — no translation needed; slicing
returns NumPy views (zero copy).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.spot_block import SpotBlock
from riskquant.portfolio.ingest.batch import IngestBatch

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from riskquant.portfolio.engine import UixMapper


def compile_spot(
    batch: IngestBatch,
    start: int,
    end: int,
    uix_mapper: UixMapper,
) -> SpotBlock | None:
    """Slice + UIX-translate a spot run from `batch[start:end]`."""
    if start == end:
        return None

    # Underlying price IDs -> market-universe UIXs.
    price_uix = uix_mapper.map_market_ids_to_uix(batch.underlying_ids[start:end])

    # FX leg (only the under->settle leg matters for spot).
    fx_spots_effective_uix = uix_mapper.map_fx_legs_to_effective_uix(
        raw_fx_ids=batch.fx_u2s_ids[start:end],
        raw_fx_inv=batch.fx_u2s_inv[start:end],
    )

    return SpotBlock(
        trade_uids=batch.uids[start:end],
        qty=batch.qty[start:end],
        price_uix=price_uix,
        fx_spots_effective_uix=fx_spots_effective_uix,
    )
```
</file>

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\portfolio\compilers\block_compilers\vanilla_european_option.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/portfolio/compilers/block_compilers/vanilla_european_option.py
"""
Vanilla European option block compilers (node and curve variants).

Translate a sub-range of an `IngestBatch` into a
`VanillaEuropeanOption*Block` via the run's `UixMapper`:
  - Underlying market ID  -> market_id_universe (price_uix / price_curve_uix)
  - Volatility market ID  -> market_id_universe (vol_uix / vol_surface_uix)
  - Discount curve IDs    -> discount_curve_id_universe
  - FX leg pairs          -> fx_effective_pairs
  - Expiry / settle / delivery ordinals -> date_ord32_universe

`qty`, `strike`, `cp_mult`, `trade_uids`, `vol_surface_y_axis_type_id` are
pure economic / typed fields — zero-copy slicing only.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.vanilla_european_option_block import (
    VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock,
    VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock,
)
from riskquant.portfolio.ingest.batch import IngestBatch

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from riskquant.portfolio.engine import UixMapper


def compile_vanilla_euro_option_node(
    batch: IngestBatch,
    start: int,
    end: int,
    uix_mapper: UixMapper,
) -> VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock | None:
    """Slice + UIX-translate a vanilla-european-option-node run."""
    if start == end:
        return None

    underlying_price_uix = uix_mapper.map_market_ids_to_uix(
        batch.underlying_ids[start:end]
    )
    vol_uix = uix_mapper.map_market_ids_to_uix(batch.volatility_ids[start:end])

    expiry_date_uix = uix_mapper.map_dates_to_uix(batch.expiry_ord[start:end])
    settlement_date_uix = uix_mapper.map_dates_to_uix(batch.settle_ord[start:end])

    fx_u2s_eff = uix_mapper.map_fx_legs_to_effective_uix(
        raw_fx_ids=batch.fx_u2s_ids[start:end],
        raw_fx_inv=batch.fx_u2s_inv[start:end],
    )
    fx_k2s_eff = uix_mapper.map_fx_legs_to_effective_uix(
        raw_fx_ids=batch.fx_k2s_ids[start:end],
        raw_fx_inv=batch.fx_k2s_inv[start:end],
    )

    under_df_uix = uix_mapper.map_discount_ids_to_uix(
        batch.under_discount_curve_ids[start:end]
    )
    strike_df_uix = uix_mapper.map_discount_ids_to_uix(
        batch.strike_discount_curve_ids[start:end]
    )

    return VanillaEuropeanOptionNodeBlock(
        trade_uids=batch.uids[start:end],
        qty=batch.qty[start:end],
        strike=batch.strike_price[start:end],
        cp_mult=batch.cp_mult[start:end],
        expiry_date_uix=expiry_date_uix,
        settlement_date_uix=settlement_date_uix,
        fx_under_to_settle_effective_uix=fx_u2s_eff,
        fx_strike_to_settle_effective_uix=fx_k2s_eff,
        under_discount_curve_uix=under_df_uix,
        strike_discount_curve_uix=strike_df_uix,
        underlying_price_uix=underlying_price_uix,
        vol_uix=vol_uix,
    )


def compile_vanilla_euro_option_curve(
    batch: IngestBatch,
    start: int,
    end: int,
    uix_mapper: UixMapper,
) -> VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock | None:
    """Slice + UIX-translate a vanilla-european-option-curve run."""
    if start == end:
        return None

    underlying_price_curve_uix = uix_mapper.map_market_ids_to_uix(
        batch.underlying_ids[start:end]
    )
    vol_surface_uix = uix_mapper.map_market_ids_to_uix(batch.volatility_ids[start:end])

    expiry_date_uix = uix_mapper.map_dates_to_uix(batch.expiry_ord[start:end])
    settlement_date_uix = uix_mapper.map_dates_to_uix(batch.settle_ord[start:end])
    delivery_date_uix = uix_mapper.map_dates_to_uix(batch.delivery_ord[start:end])

    fx_u2s_eff = uix_mapper.map_fx_legs_to_effective_uix(
        raw_fx_ids=batch.fx_u2s_ids[start:end],
        raw_fx_inv=batch.fx_u2s_inv[start:end],
    )
    fx_k2s_eff = uix_mapper.map_fx_legs_to_effective_uix(
        raw_fx_ids=batch.fx_k2s_ids[start:end],
        raw_fx_inv=batch.fx_k2s_inv[start:end],
    )

    under_df_uix = uix_mapper.map_discount_ids_to_uix(
        batch.under_discount_curve_ids[start:end]
    )
    strike_df_uix = uix_mapper.map_discount_ids_to_uix(
        batch.strike_discount_curve_ids[start:end]
    )

    return VanillaEuropeanOptionCurveBlock(
        trade_uids=batch.uids[start:end],
        qty=batch.qty[start:end],
        strike=batch.strike_price[start:end],
        cp_mult=batch.cp_mult[start:end],
        expiry_date_uix=expiry_date_uix,
        settlement_date_uix=settlement_date_uix,
        delivery_date_uix=delivery_date_uix,
        fx_under_to_settle_effective_uix=fx_u2s_eff,
        fx_strike_to_settle_effective_uix=fx_k2s_eff,
        under_discount_curve_uix=under_df_uix,
        strike_discount_curve_uix=strike_df_uix,
        underlying_price_curve_uix=underlying_price_curve_uix,
        vol_surface_uix=vol_surface_uix,
        vol_surface_y_axis_type_id=batch.vol_surface_y_axis_type_ids[start:end],
    )
```

```python
# src/riskquant/portfolio/compilers/block_compilers/vanilla_forward.py
"""
Vanilla forward block compilers (node and curve variants).

Translate a sub-range of an `IngestBatch` into a `VanillaForward*Block` via
the run's `UixMapper`:
  - Underlying market ID  -> market_id_universe        (price_uix / price_curve_uix)
  - Discount curve IDs    -> discount_curve_id_universe
  - FX leg pairs          -> fx_effective_pairs
  - Settlement / delivery ordinals -> date_ord32_universe

`qty`, `strike`, `trade_uids` are pure economics — zero-copy slicing only.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from riskquant.portfolio.blocks.vanilla_forward_block import (
    VanillaForwardCurveBlock,
    VanillaForwardNodeBlock,
)
from riskquant.portfolio.ingest.batch import IngestBatch

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from riskquant.portfolio.engine import UixMapper


def compile_vanilla_forward_node(
    batch: IngestBatch,
    start: int,
    end: int,
    uix_mapper: UixMapper,
) -> VanillaForwardNodeBlock | None:
    """Slice + UIX-translate a vanilla-forward-node run."""
    if start == end:
        return None

    price_uix = uix_mapper.map_market_ids_to_uix(batch.underlying_ids[start:end])
    settlement_date_uix = uix_mapper.map_dates_to_uix(batch.settle_ord[start:end])

    fx_u2s_eff = uix_mapper.map_fx_legs_to_effective_uix(
        raw_fx_ids=batch.fx_u2s_ids[start:end],
        raw_fx_inv=batch.fx_u2s_inv[start:end],
    )
    fx_k2s_eff = uix_mapper.map_fx_legs_to_effective_uix(
        raw_fx_ids=batch.fx_k2s_ids[start:end],
        raw_fx_inv=batch.fx_k2s_inv[start:end],
    )

    under_df_uix = uix_mapper.map_discount_ids_to_uix(
        batch.under_discount_curve_ids[start:end]
    )
    strike_df_uix = uix_mapper.map_discount_ids_to_uix(
        batch.strike_discount_curve_ids[start:end]
    )

    return VanillaForwardNodeBlock(
        trade_uids=batch.uids[start:end],
        qty=batch.qty[start:end],
        strike=batch.strike_price[start:end],
        settlement_date_uix=settlement_date_uix,
        fx_under_to_settle_effective_uix=fx_u2s_eff,
        fx_strike_to_settle_effective_uix=fx_k2s_eff,
        under_discount_curve_uix=under_df_uix,
        strike_discount_curve_uix=strike_df_uix,
        price_uix=price_uix,
    )


def compile_vanilla_forward_curve(
    batch: IngestBatch,
    start: int,
    end: int,
    uix_mapper: UixMapper,
) -> VanillaForwardCurveBlock | None:
    """Slice + UIX-translate a vanilla-forward-curve run."""
    if start == end:
        return None

    price_curve_uix = uix_mapper.map_market_ids_to_uix(batch.underlying_ids[start:end])
    settlement_date_uix = uix_mapper.map_dates_to_uix(batch.settle_ord[start:end])
    delivery_date_uix = uix_mapper.map_dates_to_uix(batch.delivery_ord[start:end])

    fx_u2s_eff = uix_mapper.map_fx_legs_to_effective_uix(
        raw_fx_ids=batch.fx_u2s_ids[start:end],
        raw_fx_inv=batch.fx_u2s_inv[start:end],
    )
    fx_k2s_eff = uix_mapper.map_fx_legs_to_effective_uix(
        raw_fx_ids=batch.fx_k2s_ids[start:end],
        raw_fx_inv=batch.fx_k2s_inv[start:end],
    )

    under_df_uix = uix_mapper.map_discount_ids_to_uix(
        batch.under_discount_curve_ids[start:end]
    )
    strike_df_uix = uix_mapper.map_discount_ids_to_uix(
        batch.strike_discount_curve_ids[start:end]
    )

    return VanillaForwardCurveBlock(
        trade_uids=batch.uids[start:end],
        qty=batch.qty[start:end],
        strike=batch.strike_price[start:end],
        settlement_date_uix=settlement_date_uix,
        delivery_date_uix=delivery_date_uix,
        fx_under_to_settle_effective_uix=fx_u2s_eff,
        fx_strike_to_settle_effective_uix=fx_k2s_eff,
        under_discount_curve_uix=under_df_uix,
        strike_discount_curve_uix=strike_df_uix,
        price_curve_uix=price_curve_uix,
    )
```
